- **copilot-measure-parity-verifier**: Fresh-context adversarial verification of a single completed copilot-measure-parity task. Dispatch after the implementer reports success, with just the task id. Reruns the verify command itself and audits for invented flags, faked capabilities (prefs teaching, growth curves, live watch, ledger role evidence on Copilot), model-id leaks into skill text, manifest/bundle drift, hand-edited copilot-docs output, fable-named content, and real-CLI or real-home touches; never trusts the implementer's claims. ([agentmc15/polytropos](https://github.com/agentmc15/polytropos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/copilot-measure-parity-verifier.md))

- **cost-hawk**: Persona reviewer for plans, specs and diffs. Use in a review panel (see /assemble-panel). ([mitcsutt/claude-toolbelt](https://github.com/mitcsutt/claude-toolbelt/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-panel/agents/cost-hawk.md))
- **cost-idempotency-reviewer**: Use this agent when adding or modifying any Lambda handler in `lambdas/&lt;svc&gt;/`. Read-only. ([reaandrew/project-pluto-old](https://github.com/reaandrew/project-pluto-old/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cost-idempotency-reviewer.md))
- **cost-monitor**: Tracks AI running cost live, enforces the confirmed cheapest-first default, and shows estimates before spending steps. Use before any potentially expensive step (parallel builders, large research passes, long test runs) and at every stage boundary. ([GRU953/GRU953-Studio](https://github.com/GRU953/GRU953-Studio/blob/HEAD/plugins/gru953-studio/agents/cost-monitor.md) · ★1)
- **cost-monitor**: Audits API and infrastructure costs across the full stack. Identifies wasteful calls, suggests caching, batching, and tier optimizations to reduce spend without degrading UX or app logic. - **cost-of-capital-analyst**: Estimates the discount rate and defends every input behind it — the riskfree rate in the mandate currency, an equity risk premium weighted by where the company actually operates, a bottom-up beta from a named comparable set, the cost of debt from the right rating table, market-value weights, and the assembled cost of equity, WACC and rate path. Delegate to it after the statements are cleaned and before any discounting happens, in any mode that needs a hurdle rate (valuation, corporate finance, acquisition, project, IPO, restructuring), and again when a critic finding targets a rate input. ([lyndonkl/claude](https://github.com/lyndonkl/claude/blob/HEAD/agents/cost-of-capital-analyst.md) · ★200)
- **cost-of-capital-build**: Builds an evidenced WACC from a sourced risk-free rate, equity risk premium, levered and unlevered beta, size and country premia, and a target capital structure, and ranks the inputs the valuation is sensitive to. Use when a Gate 2-approved valuation needs a discount rate; do not use to solve a rate back from a wanted value or approve a policy rate.
